## `ALB-2829-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ALB` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** UPW-System Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Semiconductor Wafer Megasonic Wet Bench Cleaning
- **Intro:** UPW-System Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Semiconductor Wafer Megasonic Wet Bench Cleaning — Verify Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and System Isolation: Main power supply must be OFF (0 VAC). UPW inlet valve must be fully closed (0% open). All LOTO tags must be present and dated.
- **Key tolerance:** Main power supply must be OFF (0 VAC). UPW inlet valve must be fully closed (0% open). All LOTO tags must be present and dated.
- **Standards:** `ISO 14644-1:2015`, `SEMI S2-2021 Safety Guidelines for Semiconductor Manufacturing E`, `ANSI/ASME B31.3-2020 Process Piping`, `IEC 60204-1:2016`
